HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000574/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_08597528515b56619446","text":"2. **Provision carries custodial care.** `ر ع ي/B001` denotes pasture, while dormant `ر ع ي/B002` denotes shepherding and custodial preservation. Earlier `رَبِّكَ` activates `ر ب ب/B002`, and `هَدَىٰ` activates directed care immediately before the pasture appears. The custodial branch assigns the pasture a role inside a managed relation between provider, resource, and dependent life. I infer that producing forage is an act of care. The noun becomes provision within governance rather than vegetation considered in isolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1c1fdedeaa7646680620","text":"1. **Ecological emergence under care.** Lexical evidence: خ ر ج B001/B002 (emergence/causing out), ر ع ي B001/B002 (pasture and custodial shepherding), ر ب ب B002/B008/B013 in 87:1 (nurture, cloud, collected water), ح ي ي B002 in 87:13/16 (earth alive by rain). Structural cue: pasture emerges after creation/proportion/guidance and before becoming غثاء. Assigned role: pasture is not just vegetation; it is the visible product of caring governance. Abductive move: rain/cloud/life branches explain the unseen means by which pasture comes out. Reading change: the ayah reads as a care-system, not just a botanical event.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000574/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_218d494630023d392672","text":"87:7 supplied the surprising conceptual mirror for `أَخْرَجَ`: pasture’s emergence is one material instance of crossing from hidden to manifest. Then 87:9’s benefit test retrospectively activated `ر ع ي/B003`; the pasture’s significance lies partly in watching what it becomes, not only in seeing it appear.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_43f3000f886ca84b505a","text":"1. **Pasture is disclosed from concealment.** `خ ر ج/B002` is causing something to emerge from hiddenness, and `خ ف ي/B001` versus `ج ه ر/B002` in 87:7 supplies an explicit concealed/manifest opposition. `ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ` is the concrete item moved across that boundary. I infer that its prior state is hidden in a substrate; the packet states emergence but not that location. The ayah now reads as disclosure and extraction, not merely outward motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_560072c99fdd39746b41","text":"2. **Pasture as custodial care.** Lexical evidence: رعي B002 shepherding/custodial preservation; ربب B002 nurture/completion; هدي B001 guidance. Structural cue: the same Lord who measured and guided now brings out grazing. Assigned role: مرعى serves as the material care-system for dependent creatures. Abductive move: the packet supplies shepherding and nurture branches; I infer providential care from the sequence of Lord → formation → guidance → pasture. Reading change: the ayah reads as pastoral governance, not only botanical production.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B005","root_000574/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_57caad35f075b25617a5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:4:2\",\"root_000400\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:4:3\",\"root_000574\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bulutun açılması çağrışımı, otlağın çıkışını yukarıdan gelen suyla aşağıdaki büyümeyi birleştiren dikey bir düzen içine yerleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_65afe35f54e893f04cbe","text":"87:5 immediately overturned any simple abundance reading: the same pasture becomes غثاء أحوى. 87:16-17 later made the pasture cycle a sign against choosing near, perishing life over what remains.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000574/B001","root_000574/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7521cbc043d4a9de9df7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:4:3\",\"root_000574\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Otlak, bağımlı canlıları gözetip besleyen bir bakım düzeninin maddi aracı niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7a4f1d0b96886c12548e","text":"1. **Pasture as extracted life from hidden interior.** Lexical evidence: خرج B002 causing something to come out; رعي B001 grazing/pasture; خفي B002 hidden/inner things and B003 bringing out in 87:7. Structural cue: \"أخرج\" is followed by the object \"المرعى\", and hidden/open knowledge appears soon after. Assigned role: pasture becomes visible provision pulled from concealment. Abductive move: the packet gives emergence and concealment; I infer soil/hiddenness as the unseen reservoir. Reading change: pasture is not merely vegetation; it is disclosure of stored life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_81c883f61f50c10a50c7","text":"3. **Emergence can also be yield and obligation.** Lexical evidence: خرج B003 due output of wealth/yield; رعي B006 preserving a claim. Structural cue: after created provision, the surah later asks humans to remember, purify, and not prefer الدنيا. Assigned role: pasture as \"output\" carries a claim on the recipient. Abductive move: the packet gives yield/claim branches; I infer moral accountability from later purification and preference language. Reading change: provision is not neutral consumption; it is a yielded trust that should activate remembrance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000574/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_984d62165d12d601bc9a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:4:3\",\"root_000574\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Otlağın hemen ardından dönüşmesi, onu yalnız bolluk anı olarak değil, sonucu izlenen bir hayat evresi olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9ff719564d0bd8d10014","text":"3. **Public yield.** Lexical evidence: خ ر ج B003 (due output/yield), ر ع ي B003/B006 (watching outcome and preserving claim), ن ف ع B001 in 87:9. Structural cue: the pasture is later tested by benefit and use. Assigned role: المرعى is a yield offered to creatures, and reminder is a yield offered to hearers. Abductive move: what comes out has value only if it is received and used. Reading change: emergence carries an implied accountability of benefit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B003","root_000574/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a17e9873c20768c8ceb5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:4:2\",\"root_000400\",[\"B003\"]],[\"87:4:3\",\"root_000574\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Otlağın bir çıktı ve korunması gereken hak gibi okunması, verilen rızkı tüketilecek maldan sorumluluk taşıyan emanete çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aa95ceb79698959b230b","text":"87:9 made the pasture scene less decorative: \"if the reminder benefits\" activates pasture as useful yield. 87:7's hidden/declared pair also made إخراج look like disclosure, not only growth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_af19d5bc20a0a6b26907","text":"4. **Cloud/sky ecology activates overhead naming.** Lexical evidence: سمو B004 overhead sky/cloud/rain/vegetation; ربب B008 layered cloud nurturing plants; خرج B005 cloud emergence/clearing; رعي B001 pasture. Structural cue: name/elevation in 87:1 and pasture in 87:4 meet through rain-cloud branches. Assigned role: the upper realm is materially involved in lower growth. Abductive move: the packet supplies cloud/rain/vegetation links; I infer a vertical ecology from الأعلى to المرعى. Reading change: \"brought out pasture\" becomes a sky-earth mechanism under the Highest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e35fe649963540474039","text":"2. **Revelation/reminder as bringing out what is hidden.** Lexical evidence: خ ر ج B002/B010 (extracting/bringing out hidden), خ ف ي B003 in 87:7 (removing concealment), ق ر ء B001 in 87:6 (gathering speech), ذ ك ر B003/B009 in 87:9. Structural cue: 87:6-9 move from recitation to hidden/declared knowledge to reminder. Assigned role: pasture emergence becomes an analogy for latent memory being brought into the open. Abductive move: the same out-from-hidden motion can govern plants and meanings. Reading change: \"brings out pasture\" foreshadows bringing out recall and admonition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000400/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_edd88688cee44f03834c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:4:2\",\"root_000400\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Otlağın ortaya çıkışı, saklı bir iç alandaki hayatın görünür kılınması olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000574/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f28cec17ff47c8a49efc","text":"3. **The pasture is presented under outcome-surveillance.** `ر ع ي/B003` covers watching an affair and considering how it will turn out. Its immediate conversion in 87:5 and the usefulness test attached to reminder in 87:9 make outcome, rather than initial appearance, the structural focus. This branch makes `ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ` an object whose trajectory must be watched. I infer that the fast sequel invites that monitoring stance. The ayah changes from a snapshot of abundance into the opening phase of a tracked lifecycle.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
